Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the statistical_mechanics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 221. The resulting operator achieves bound O(982/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the statistical_mechanics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^221 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 982-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
